The substitution infrastructure compiles. Now the logical relation V, E, env_rel, V_high, V_to_E, env_rel_update. Then V_subtype, transparent_ground_erase, fundamental, and the final theorem.

Let me insert the V/E definitions and basic lemmas before the Theorem.

I'll write:

```coq
(* ============================================================== *)
(* The logical relation                                            *)
(* ============================================================== *)

Fixpoint V (T : ty) (v1 v2 : tm) {struct T} : Prop :=
  match T with
  | Ty_Unit k =>
      match k.(indirect_reader) with
      | High => True
      | Low => exists k1 k2, v1 = tm_unit k1 /\ v2 = tm_unit k2 /\
                 security_le k1 k /\ security_le k2 k
      end
  | Ty_Sum T1 T2 k =>
      match k.(indirect_reader) with
      | High => True
      | Low =>
          (exists a1 a2 ka1 ka2,
             v1 = tm_inl a1 ka1 /\ v2 = tm_inl a2 ka2 /\
             security_le ka1 k /\ security_le ka2 k /\ V T1 a1 a2)
          \/
          (exists a1 a2 ka1 ka2,
             v1 = tm_inr a1 ka1 /\ v2 = tm_inr a2 ka2 /\
             security_le ka1 k /\ security_le ka2 k /\ V T2 a1 a2)
      end
  | Ty_Prod T1 T2 k =>
      match k.(indirect_reader) with
      | High => True
      | Low =>
          exists a1 a2 b1 b2 ka1 ka2,
            v1 = tm_pair a1 b1 ka1 /\ v2 = tm_pair a2 b2 ka2 /\
            security_le ka1 k /\ security_le ka2 k /\ V T1 a1 a2 /\ V T2 b1 b2
      end
  | Ty_Arrow T1 T2 k =>
      match k.(indirect_reader) with
      | High => True
      | Low =>
          exists Ta1 body1 ka1 Ta2 body2 ka2,
            v1 = tm_abs Ta1 body1 ka1 /\ v2 = tm_abs Ta2 body2 ka2 /\
            security_le ka1 k /\ security_le ka2 k /\
            (forall w1 w2 r, value w1 -> value w2 -> V T1 w1 w2 ->
               flows_to k.(reader) r ->
               forall u1 u2,
                 tm_app v1 w1 r -->* u1 -> value u1 ->
                 tm_app v2 w2 r -->* u2 -> value u2 ->
                 V T2 u1 u2)
      end
  end.

Definition E (T : ty) (t1 t2 : tm) : Prop :=
  forall v1 v2, t1 -->* v1 -> value v1 -> t2 -->* v2 -> value v2 -> V T v1 v2.

Lemma V_high : forall T v1 v2,
  (security_of T).(indirect_reader) = High -> V T v1 v2.
Proof.
  intros T v1 v2 H.
  destruct T; simpl in H |- *; rewrite H; exact I.
Qed.
```
Hmm, `simpl in H |- *` in destruct branches: After destruct T:
- Ty_Unit s: H : (security_of (Ty_Unit s)).ind = High. simpl in H might reduce security_of to s? security_of (Ty_Unit s) is match Ty_Unit s with ... => s end = s; simpl reduces to s. So H : s.ind = High. Goal V (Ty_Unit s) v1 v2; simpl reduces to match s.ind with High=>True|Low=>... end. rewrite H; exact I. Good.
- Sum/Prod/Arrow similar.
But `simpl in H |- *` might not unfold V because V is a Fixpoint requiring the match on the type; simpl should. Let's test.

```coq
Lemma V_to_E_value : forall T v1 v2,
  value v1 -> value v2 -> V T v1 v2 -> E T v1 v2.
Proof.
  intros T v1 v2 Hv1 Hv2 HV u1 u2 Hm1 Hval1 Hm2 Hval2.
  assert (Hu1 : u1 = v1) by (eapply multi_value_eq; eauto).
  assert (Hu2 : u2 = v2) by (eapply multi_value_eq; eauto).
  subst u1; subst u2. exact HV.
Qed.
```
The eapply multi_value_eq; eauto: multi_value_eq v u : multi v u -> value v -> value u -> u = v. For Hu1: eapply with u=u1, v=v1; goals multi v1 u1 (Hm1), value v1 (Hv1), value u1 (Hval1). eauto should solve. Alternatively exact (multi_value_eq v1 u1 Hm1 Hv1 Hval1).

```coq
Definition env_rel (Gamma : context) (sigma1 sigma2 : env) : Prop :=
  (forall y, locally_closed (sigma1 y)) /\
  (forall y, locally_closed (sigma2 y)) /\
  (forall y T, lookup_context y Gamma = Some T -> E T (sigma1 y) (sigma2 y)).

Lemma env_rel_update : forall Gamma sigma1 sigma2 x T w1 w2,
  env_rel Gamma sigma1 sigma2 ->
  value w1 -> value w2 -> V T w1 w2 ->
  env_rel (update Gamma x T) (env_update sigma1 x w1) (env_update sigma2 x w2).
Proof.
  intros Gamma sigma1 sigma2 x T w1 w2 (Hlc1 & Hlc2 & HE) Hv1 Hv2 HV.
  split.
  - intros y. unfold env_update. destruct (Nat.eqb y x); [apply value_lc; auto | apply Hlc1].
  - split.
    + intros y. unfold env_update. destruct (Nat.eqb y x); [apply value_lc; auto | apply Hlc2].
    + intros y U Hlook. unfold update in Hlook.
      destruct (Nat.eqb y x) eqn:Ey.
      * apply Nat.eqb_eq in Ey. subst y.
        injection Hlook as HU. subst U.
        unfold env_update. rewrite Nat.eqb_refl.
        apply V_to_E_value; auto.
      * unfold env_update. rewrite Ey.
        apply HE. exact Hlook.
Qed.
```
Careful: `unfold update in Hlook` — update is `(x,T)::Gamma`; lookup_context y ((x,T)::Gamma) = if eqb y x then Some T else lookup y Gamma. `simpl in Hlook` might be needed. After destruct (eqb y x) eqn:Ey, Hlook in the true branch : Some T = Some U (if the if reduced). We should `simpl in Hlook` after unfold. Let me write `unfold update in Hlook; simpl in Hlook.` Then destruct.

In the true branch, Ey : (y =? x) = true. apply Nat.eqb_eq in Ey; subst y. injection Hlook as HU. subst U. Goal E T (env_update sigma1 x w1 x) (env_update sigma2 x w2 x) = E T w1 w2. unfold env_update; rewrite Nat.eqb_refl twice? The goal has two occurrences. simpl should reduce both. Use `simpl` after unfold. Then apply V_to_E_value; auto. But V_to_E_value expects value w1, value w2, V T w1 w2. auto uses Hv1,Hv2,HV. ✓.
In false branch: Hlook : lookup_context y Gamma = Some U. Goal E U (env_update sigma1 x w1 y) (env_update sigma2 x w2 y). unfold env_update; rewrite Ey (both occurrences). apply HE; exact Hlook. ✓.

```coq
Lemma V_subtype : forall T U, subtype T U -> forall v1 v2, V T v1 v2 -> V U v1 v2.
Proof.
  intros T U Hsub.
  induction Hsub as
    [ k1 k2 Hw1 Hw2 Hle
    | T1 T2 U1 U2 k1 k2 Hs1 Hs2 Hw1 Hw2 Hle IH1 IH2
    | T1 T2 U1 U2 k1 k2 Hs1 Hs2 Hw1 Hw2 Hle IH1 IH2
    | T1 T2 U1 U2 k1 k2 Hs1 Hs2 Hw1 Hw2 Hle IH1 IH2
    | T U0 V0 Hs1 Hs2 IH1 IH2 ];
    intros v1 v2 HV.

Lemma transparent_ground_erase : forall kappa T v1 v2,
  transparent_at kappa T -> ground T ->
  flows_to kappa.(indirect_reader) Low ->

Now the fundamental theorem. This is the biggest piece. Let me write it.

```coq
Lemma fundamental : forall Gamma t T,
  has_type Gamma t T ->
  forall sigma1 sigma2, env_rel Gamma sigma1 sigma2 ->
  E T (subst_env sigma1 t) (subst_env sigma2 t).
Proof.
  intros Gamma t T Hty.
  induction Hty as
    [ Gamma x T0 Hlook Hwf
    | Gamma kappa Hwf
    | L Gamma T1 body T2 kappa HwfT1 Hwfkappa Hbody IHbody
    | Gamma t1 t2 T1 T2 kappa r Ht1 Ht2 Hflow IH1 IH2
    | Gamma t1 t2 T1 T2 kappa Ht1 Ht2 Hwf IH1 IH2
    | Gamma t T1 T2 kappa r Ht Hflow IH
    | Gamma t T1 T2 kappa r Ht Hflow IH
    | Gamma t T1 T2 kappa Ht HwfT2 Hwfkappa IH
    | Gamma t T1 T2 kappa HwfT1 Ht Hwfkappa IH
    | L Gamma t body1 body2 T1 T2 T0 kappa r Ht Hflow Hbody1 Hbody2 IHt IH1 IH2
    | Gamma t T0 l Ht IH
    | Gamma t T0 U Ht Hsub IH
    | Gamma c yes no k T0 r Hc Hyes Hno Hflow IHc IHyes IHno ];
    intros sigma1 sigma2 Henv.
```
Need to check the induction premise interleaving: for each constructor, IHs come right after recursive premises. Let's list the constructors and their premises:

T_Var : forall Gamma x T, lookup_context x Gamma = Some T -> wf_ty T -> has_type Gamma (tm_fvar x) T. No recursive. Pattern: `Gamma x T0 Hlook Hwf`.
T_Unit : Gamma kappa, wf_security kappa. Pattern `Gamma kappa Hwf`.
T_Abs : forall (L:list atom) Gamma T1 body T2 kappa, wf_ty T1 -> wf_security kappa -> (forall x, ~In x L -> has_type (update Gamma x T1) (open body (fvar x)) T2) -> has_type Gamma (tm_abs T1 body kappa) (Ty_Arrow T1 T2 kappa).
The recursive premise is the function; the IH comes after it: pattern `L Gamma T1 body T2 kappa HwfT1 Hwfkappa Hbody IHbody` where IHbody : forall x Hx, <motive at the premise>. Our induction's motive is `fun Gamma t T => forall sigma1 sigma2, env_rel Gamma sigma1 sigma2 -> E T (subst sigma1 t) (subst sigma2 t)`. So IHbody : forall x (Hx : ~In x L), forall sigma1 sigma2, env_rel (update Gamma x T1) sigma1 sigma2 -> E T2 (subst sigma1 (open body (fvar x))) (subst sigma2 (...)).
T_App : Gamma t1 t2 T1 T2 kappa r, has_type Gamma t1 (Arrow T1 T2 kappa) -> has_type Gamma t2 T1 -> flows_to kappa.reader r -> has_type Gamma (app t1 t2 r) (ty_protect kappa.ind T2). Pattern: `Gamma t1 t2 T1 T2 kappa r Ht1 IH1 Ht2 IH2 Hflow`. Note the order of premises in the constructor: has_type t1 ..., has_type t2 ..., flows_to... So IHs after each recursive: `Ht1 IH1 Ht2 IH2 Hflow`.
T_Pair: Gamma t1 t2 T1 T2 kappa, has_type t1 T1 -> has_type t2 T2 -> wf_security kappa. Pattern `Ht1 IH1 Ht2 IH2 Hwf`.
T_Fst: Gamma t T1 T2 kappa r, has_type t (Ty_Prod T1 T2 kappa) -> flows_to kappa.reader r. Pattern `Ht IH Hflow`.
T_Snd same.
T_Inl: Gamma t T1 T2 kappa, has_type t T1 -> wf_ty T2 -> wf_security kappa. Pattern `Ht IH HwfT2 Hwfkappa`.
T_Inr: Gamma t T1 T2 kappa, wf_ty T1 -> has_type t T2 -> wf_security kappa. Pattern `HwfT1 Ht IH Hwfkappa`.
T_Case: forall L Gamma t body1 body2 T1 T2 T kappa r, has_type Gamma t (Sum T1 T2 kappa) -> flows_to kappa.reader r -> (forall x, ~In x L -> has_type (update Gamma x T1) (open body1 (fvar x)) T) -> (forall x, ~In x L -> has_type (update Gamma x T2) (open body2 (fvar x)) T) -> has_type Gamma (case t body1 body2 r) (ty_protect kappa.ind T).
Pattern: `L Gamma t body1 body2 T1 T2 T0 kappa r Ht IHt Hflow Hbody1 IH1 Hbody2 IH2`.
T_Protect: Gamma t T0 l, has_type t T0 -> has_type Gamma (protect l t) (ty_protect l T0). Pattern `Gamma t T0 l Ht IH`.
T_Sub: Gamma t T0 U, has_type t T0 -> subtype T0 U. Pattern `Gamma t T0 U Ht IH Hsub`.
T_If: forall Gamma c yes no k T r, has_type Gamma c (Sum (Unit public)(Unit public) k) -> has_type Gamma yes T -> has_type Gamma no T -> flows_to k.reader r -> has_type Gamma (if c yes no r) (ty_protect k.ind T). Pattern `Gamma c yes no k T0 r Hc IHc Hyes IHyes Hno IHno Hflow`.

```
Fixpoint max_list (l : list nat) : nat :=
  match l with [] => 0 | y :: ys => max (S y) (max_list ys) end.
Lemma max_list_gt : forall l y, In y l -> y < max_list l.
Proof.
  induction l as [| z zs IH]; simpl; intros y Hy; [contradiction|].
  destruct Hy as [-> | Hy].
  - apply Nat.lt_le_trans with (S y); [lia | apply Nat.le_max_l].
  - apply Nat.lt_le_trans with (max_list zs); [apply IH; auto | apply Nat.le_max_r].
Qed.
Definition fresh_atom (l : list atom) : atom := max_list l.
Lemma fresh_atom_notin : forall l x, In x l -> x < fresh_atom l.
Proof. intros; apply max_list_gt; auto. Qed.
```

  locally_closed w ->

T_Case:

- High: V_high + ty_protect_high_ind.

T_Protect:
Result ty_protect l T0. Case l:
- l = Low: ty_protect_low; E T0. Evaluations of tm_protect Low (subst t0). protect_multi_decomp gives s -->* u', value u', v1 = protect_value u' Low = u'. Then IH gives E T0 (subst t0); apply to evaluations → V T0 u'1 u'2; rewrite v1=u'1, v2=u'2. ✓.
- l = High: V_high + ty_protect_high_ind.

T_Sub:
Goal E U (subst t) (subst t). `unfold E; intros v1 v2 ...; apply V_subtype with (T := T0); [exact Hsub | apply (IH sigma1 sigma2 Henv) ...]`. IH : E T0 ...; apply IH to evaluations gives V T0; then V_subtype.

T_If:
Result ty_protect k.ind T0. Case k.ind:
- High: V_high + ty_protect_high_ind.
- Low: ty_protect_low → E T0.
  Evaluations of tm_if (subst c) (subst yes) (subst no) r.
  if_multi_decomp gives c' with subst c -->* c', value c', multi (if c' ...) v1.
  IHc gives E (Sum (Unit public)(Unit public) k) ...; apply → V sum.
  Low branch (k.ind = Low, current) → V sum strict: c1' = inl a1 ka1 or inr; matching.
  inl: value a1; flow ka1.reader ≤ k.reader ≤ r; if_inl_multi → multi (protect ka1.ind (subst yes)) v1; ka1.ind Low; protect decomp → yes evaluates to u1' with v1=u1'. IHyes gives E T0 (subst yes) (subst no); apply → V T0 u1' u2'. rewrite.
  inr similarly with IHno.

Lemma V_subtype : forall T U, subtype T U -> forall v1 v2, V T v1 v2 -> V U v1 v2.
Proof.
  intros T U Hsub.
  induction Hsub as
    [ k1 k2 Hw1 Hw2 Hle

        -- right. ...

  - Prod similarly.
  - Arrow similarly.
  - Trans.
Qed.

```coq
Lemma transparent_ground_erase : forall kappa T v1 v2,
  transparent_at kappa T -> ground T ->
  flows_to kappa.(indirect_reader) Low ->
  V T v1 v2 -> erase_security v1 = erase_security v2.
Proof.
  intros kappa T.
  induction T as [k | T1 IH1 T2 IH2 k | T1 IH1 T2 IH2 k | T1 IH1 T2 IH2 k];
    intros v1 v2 Htr Hg Hflow HV.
  - simpl in Hg. (* True *)
    destruct Htr as [Hle _].
    assert (Hk : k.(indirect_reader) = Low).
    { destruct (k.(indirect_reader)) eqn:E; auto.
      exfalso. destruct Hle as [_ Hf].
      exact (flows_to_trans _ _ _ Hf Hflow). }
    simpl in HV. rewrite Hk in HV. simpl in HV.
    destruct HV as (k1 & k2 & Hv1 & Hv2 & _ & _).
    rewrite Hv1, Hv2. simpl. reflexivity.
  - simpl in Hg. destruct Hg as [Hg1 Hg2].
    destruct Htr as [Hle [Htr1 Htr2]].
    assert (Hk : k.(indirect_reader) = Low).
    { destruct (k.(indirect_reader)) eqn:E; auto.
      exfalso. destruct Hle as [_ Hf].
      exact (flows_to_trans _ _ _ Hf Hflow). }
    simpl in HV. rewrite Hk in HV. simpl in HV.
    destruct HV as [(a1 & a2 & ka1 & ka2 & Hv1 & Hv2 & _ & _ & HVp) |
                   (a1 & a2 & ka1 & ka2 & Hv1 & Hv2 & _ & _ & HVp)].
    + rewrite Hv1, Hv2. simpl. f_equal. apply IH1; auto.
    + rewrite Hv1, Hv2. simpl. f_equal. apply IH2; auto.
  - simpl in Hg. destruct Hg as [Hg1 Hg2].
    destruct Htr as [Hle [Htr1 Htr2]].
    assert (Hk : k.(indirect_reader) = Low).
    { destruct (k.(indirect_reader)) eqn:E; auto.
      exfalso. destruct Hle as [_ Hf].
      exact (flows_to_trans _ _ _ Hf Hflow). }

    destruct HV as (a1 & a2 & b1 & b2 & ka1 & ka2 & Hv1 & Hv2 & _ & _ & HV1 & HV2).
    rewrite Hv1, Hv2. simpl. f_equal.
    + apply IH1; auto.
    + apply IH2; auto.
  - simpl in Hg. destruct Hg.
Qed.
```

```coq
Lemma fundamental : forall Gamma t T,
  has_type Gamma t T ->
  forall sigma1 sigma2, env_rel Gamma sigma1 sigma2 ->
  E T (subst_env sigma1 t) (subst_env sigma2 t).
Proof.
  intros Gamma t T Hty.
  induction Hty as
    [ Gamma x T0 Hlook Hwf
    | Gamma kappa Hwf
    | L Gamma T1 body T2 kappa HwfT1 Hwfkappa Hbody IHbody
    | Gamma t1 t2 T1 T2 kappa r Ht1 IH1 Ht2 IH2 Hflow
    | Gamma t1 t2 T1 T2 kappa Ht1 IH1 Ht2 IH2 Hwf
    | Gamma t T1 T2 kappa r Ht IH Hflow
    | Gamma t T1 T2 kappa r Ht IH Hflow
    | Gamma t T1 T2 kappa Ht IH HwfT2 Hwfkappa
    | Gamma t T1 T2 kappa HwfT1 Ht IH Hwfkappa
    | L Gamma t b1 b2 T1 T2 T0 kappa r Ht IHt Hflow Hb1 IH1 Hb2 IH2
    | Gamma t T0 l Ht IH
    | Gamma t T0 U Ht IH Hsub
    | Gamma c yes no k T0 r Hc IHc Hyes IHyes Hno IHno Hflow ];
    intros sigma1 sigma2 Henv.
```

Finally the main theorem:

```
Theorem noninterference : ... .
Proof.
  intros C t1 t2 T_secret T_result Hty1 Hty2 Hctx Hg Htr Hnof.
  destruct Hctx as (L & Hctx).
  assert (Hres : (security_of T_result).(indirect_reader) = Low).
  { destruct ((security_of T_result).(indirect_reader)) eqn:E; auto.
    exfalso. apply Hnof. rewrite E. simpl. exact I. }
  assert (Hsec : (security_of T_secret).(indirect_reader) = High).
  { destruct ((security_of T_secret).(indirect_reader)) eqn:E.
    - exfalso. apply Hnof. rewrite E, Hres. simpl. exact I.
    - reflexivity. }
```
Careful with destruct order: first branch Low, second High. In the Low branch (E : T_secret.ind = Low), apply Hnof; rewrite E, Hres: Hnof needs flows_to Low Low; simpl gives True; exact I. In High branch, reflexivity. ✓.

```
  set (x := fresh_atom (L ++ fv_context C)).
  assert (HxL : ~ In x L).
  { intro Hx. apply (fresh_atom_fresh (L ++ fv_context C)). apply in_or_app; auto. }
  assert (HxC : ~ In x (fv_context C)).
  { intro Hx. apply (fresh_atom_fresh (L ++ fv_context C)). apply in_or_app; auto. }
  assert (Htyx : has_type (update empty x T_secret) (plug C (tm_fvar x)) T_result)
    by (apply Hctx; exact HxL).
```
Wait, x is set; the membership In x L uses x convertible to fresh_atom...; fine.

Define sigma0, sigma1, sigma2:
```
  set (sigma1 := env_update (fun y : atom => tm_fvar y) x t1).
  set (sigma2 := env_update (fun y : atom => tm_fvar y) x t2).
  assert (Hsigma1x : sigma1 x = t1) by (unfold sigma1; apply env_update_same).
